           The Group has organised its operations into four main                                          本集團的經營分為四項主要營運分部：
           operating segments:

           (1)    F&F and Food ingredients;                                                               (1)   香精及食品配料；

           (2)    Tobacco raw materials;                                                                  (2)   煙用原料；

           (3)    Aroma raw materials; and                                                                (3)   香原料；及

           (4)    Condiment.                                                                              (4)   調味品。

           The chief operating decision-makers have been identified                                       主要經營決策者明確為執行董事（「執行
           as the executive directors (the “Executive Directors”).                                        董事」），執行董事審閱本集團的內部報
           T h e E x e c u t i v e D i r e c t o r s r e v i e w t h e G r o u p’s i n t e r n a l        告以評估表現及分配資源。管理層按照
           reporting in order to assess performance and allocate                                          此報告以決定營運分部。
           resources. Management has determined the operating
           segments based on these reports.

           The Executive Directors consider the business from the                                         執行董事從經營活動角度考慮業務及評
           operation’s perspective and assess the performance of                                          估 香 精 及 食 品 配 料、 煙 用 原 料、 香 原 料

                  products, and food ingredient products.                                                       品。

                 The Company’s management reviews the provision                     本公司管理層持續審查商譽的減值

                 generally annually and more frequently if there are                任何可能導致潛在減值的事件或
                 any events or changes in circumstances that would                  情 況 變 化， 則 審 查 頻 率 會 相 應 提
                 give rise to potential impairment. The Company                     高。 本 公 司 採 用 收 益 法（亦 即 貼 現
                 uses the income approach (i.e. discounted cash                     現 金 流 量 模 型 ）作 為 有 關 計 提 的 評
                 flow models) as the evaluation method for such                     估方法。採用這種方法的原因乃根
                 provision. The reason for adopting this method is                  據《香 港 會 計 準 則 第 36 號 － 資 產 減
                 that according to HKAS 36 — Impairment of Assets,                  值 》， 商 譽 減 值 評 估 通 常 採 用 收 益
                 goodwill impairment assessment usually uses the                    法來確定 CGU 之價值。具有足夠長
                 income approach to determine the value of a CGU.                   時間之財務歷史的 CGU 則相應具備
                 CGU that have a sufficiently long financial history                預測未來業績的基礎，在這種情況
                 would have a basis for predicting future results,                  下，CGU 之商譽的可收回金額將基
                 and in such case, the recoverable amount of                        於 使 用 價 值（「VIU」）而 定。 由 於 集
                 goodwill of the CGU would be based on the value                    團 各 個 CGUs 均 為 具 有 較 長 時 間 之
                 in use (“VIU”). As each of the Group’s CGUs is CGU                 財務歷史的 CGU、具備預測其未來
                 having a long financial history which could provide                業績的基礎，故滿足使用貼現現金
                 the basis for predicting its future results, it satisfied          流量模型的條件，因此可以根據歷
                 the criteria for the application of the discounted                 史數據進行其收益的預測。
                 cash flow models and hence its profitability forecast
                 could be conducted based on historical data.
